का०मा० 3041 .----नोटरीज नियम , 1956 के नियम 6 के 
अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी द्वारा यह सूचना दी जाती है कि 
श्री सोम प्रकाश धवन, एडवोकेट दिल्ली ने उक्त प्राधिकारी को 
उक्त नियम के नियम 4 के अधीन एक आवेदन इस बात के 
लिए दिना है कि उसे पटियाला हाउस , नई दिल्ली में 
पावसाय करने के लिए नोटरी के रूप में नियुक्त किया 
जाए । 

2 . उक्त व्यक्ति को नोटरी के रूप में नियुक्ति पर 
किसी भी प्रकार का आक्षेप इस सूचना के प्रकाशन के चौदह 
दिन के भोतर लिखित रूप में मेरे पास भेजा जाए । 

[ सं० फा० 5 ( 54)/ 83- न्या ०] 


का० आ० 3042. -- नोटरीज नियम , 1956 के नियम 
6 के अनुसरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी द्वारा यह सूचना दी 
जाती है कि श्री राजवीर सिंह , एडवोकेट दिल्ली ने उक्त 
प्राधिकारी को उक्त नियम के नियम 4 के अधीन एक 
आवेदन इस बात के लिए दिया है कि उसे शाहदरा 
कोर्ट दिल्ली में व्यवमाय करने के लिए नोटरी के रूप में 
नियुक्त किया जाए । 


2. उक्त व्यक्ति को नोटरी के रूप में नियुक्ति पर 
किसी भी प्रकार का प्राक्षेप इस सूचना के प्रकाशन के चौदह 
दिन के भीतर लिखित रूप में मेरे पास भेजा जाए ।

S. 0 . 3045 . - In exercise of the powers conferred by section 
3 of the Delhi Special Police Establishment Act, 1946 ( 25 
of 1946) , the Central Government hereby specifies the follow 
ing offences as offences which are to be investigated by the 
Delhi Special Police Establishment, namely : -- 


( a ) Offences punishable under the Unlawful 

( Prevention ) Act , 1967 ( 37 of 1967) . 


Activitics 


( b ) Attempts, abetments and conspiracles in relation to , 

or in connection with , one or more of the offences 
mentioned above , and any other offenco committed 
in the course of the same transaction arising out 
of the same facts .

नई दिल्ली , 15 जुलाई, 1983 
का० आ० 3053: - आयकर अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1961 
का 43 ) की धारा 121क की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और जारी की गयी पूर्ववर्ती 
अधिसूचनाओं में आंशिक संशोधन करते हुए केन्द्र प्रत्यक्ष 
कर बोर्ड एतद्द्वारा निदेश देता है कि नीचे दी गई अनुसूची 
के स्तम्भ ( 1 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट अधिकार - क्षेत्रों के आयकर आयुक्त 
( अपील ) अनुसूची के स्तम्भ ( 2 ) की तत्संबंधी प्रविष्टियों में 
विनिर्दिष्ट आयकर वाडौं, परिमंडलों , जिलों और रेंजों में 
ऐसे व्यक्तियों के संबंध में अपने कार्य करेंगे जिन पर आयकर 
या अतिकर या व्याजकर लगाया गया हो और जो आयकर 
अधिनियम , 1961 की धारा 246 की उपधारा ( 2 ) के 
खण्ड ( क ) से ( ज ) में कंपनी ( लाभ ) अतिकर अधिनियम , 
1964 ( 1964 का 7 ) की धारा 11 को उपधारा ( 1 ) 
में और ब्याजकर अधिनियम, 1974 ( 1974 का 45 ) 
की धारा 15 की उपधारा ( 1 ) में उल्लिखित किसी भी 
आदेश से व्यथित हुए हैं और ऐसे व्यक्तियों या व्यक्तियों के 
वर्गों की बाबत भी कार्य करेगें जिनके लिए बोर्ड ने आयकर 
अधिनियम , 1961 की धारा 246 की उपधारा ( 2 ) के 
खण्ड ( 1 ) के उपबंधों के अनुसार निदेश दिया है या भविष्य 
में निदेश में । 


यह अधिसूचना 15 अप्रैल , 1983 से प्रभावी होगी । 
[ सं० 5157/फा० सं० 189/ 5 / 81- आ० क० (नि० - I )] 

मिलाप जैन , अवर सचिव ,

जहां कोई आयकर वार्ड, परिमंडल , जिला अथवा रेंज 
अथवा उसका कोई भाग इस अधिसूचना द्वारा एक अधिकार 
क्षेत्र से अन्य अधिकार- क्षेत्र में अंतरित कर दिया गया है , 
यहां आयकर धार्ड , परिमंडल, जिला अथवा रेंज अथवा उसके 
किसी भाग में किए गए कर-निर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाली 
और इस अधिसूचना से तत्काल पूर्व उस अधिकार क्षेत्र के 
समक्ष विचाराधीन पड़ी अपीलें , जिसके अधिकार क्षेत्र से यह 
आयकर वार्ड, परिमंडल, जिला अथवा रेंज अथवा उनका कोई 
भाग अंतरित किया गया है, इस अधिसूचना के लागू होने 
की तारीख से उस आयकर आयुक्त ( अपील ) के अधिकार . 
क्षेत्र को अंतरित की जायेंगी और उसके द्वारा निपटायी 
जायेगी, जिसके अधिकार- क्षेत्र में उक्त वार्ड , परिमंडल , 
जिला अथवा रेंज अथवा उसका कोई भाग अंतरित कर 
दिया गया है ।

S . O . 3053 . — In exercise of the powers conferred 
by sub - section ( 1 ) of sec . ion 121A of the Income-tax 
Act, 1961 (43 of 1961) and in partial modification 
of the notifications issued earlier , the Central Board of 
Direct Taxes hereby directs that the Commissioners 
of Income- tax (Appeals ) of the charges specified in 
column No. (1 ) of the schedule below, shall perform 
their functions in respect of such persons assessed to 
Income- tax or Surtax or interest tax in the Income tax 
Wards, Circles, Districts and Ranges specified in the 
corresponding entries in column 2 thereof as are 
aggrieved by any of the orders mentioned in clauses 
( a ) to ( h ) of sub -section ( 2 ) of section 246 of the 
Income- tax Act, 1961, in sub - section ( 1 ) of section 11 
of Companies ( Profits ) Sur- tax Act , 1964 ( 7 of 1964 ) 
and in sub - section ( 1 ) of section 15 of the Interest 
Tax Act, 1974 (45 of 1974 ) and also in respect of such 
persons or classes of persons as the Board has directed 
or may direct in future in accordance with the 
provisions of clause ( 1) of sub - section ( 2) of section 246 
of the Income - tax Act, 1961 .

Whereas Income- tax Ward , Circle, District or 
Range or part thereof stands transferred by this 
Notification from one charge to another charge , 
appeals arising out of assessment made in that 
Income- tax Ward , Circle, District or Range or part 
thereof and pending immediately before the date of 
this Notification before the Commissioner of Income 
tax (Appeals ) of the charge from whom the Income 
tax Ward , Circle , District or Range or part thereof 
is transferred , are to be dealt with by the Commissioner 
of Income- tax (Appeals ) of the charge to whom the 
said Ward , Circle District or Range or part thereof 
is transferred

सामने निम्नलिखित को मद ( VI ) के रूप में जोड़ा जाएगा:- --

का० आ० 3059 : -~- आयकर अधिनियम 1961 
( 1961 का 43 ) की धारा 122 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए तथा इस विषय 
· को पिछले अधिसूचनाओं का अधिलंघन करते हुए केन्द्रीय 
प्रत्यक्ष कर बोर्ड एतद्द्वारा निदेश देता है कि निम्नलिखित 
अनुसूची के सम्भ ( 2 ) में विनिदिष्ट रेंजों के अपीलीय 
सहायक आयकर आयक आयकर से निर्धारित उन सभी 
व्यक्तियों और आय को छोड़कर जिन पर अधिकारिता 
आयकर आयुक्त ( अपील ) में निहित है , अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 
3 की तत्संबंधी प्रविष्टि में विनिर्दिष्ट आयकर परिमंडलों 
वार्डो और जिलों में आयकर से निर्धारित सभी व्यक्तियों 
और आय के संबंध में अपने कार्य का निर्वहण करेंगे : - -- 

अनुसूची 
प्रा० सं० रेंज आयकर परिमंडल / वार्ड /जिले 

- - -- - - - 
12 
1 . अपीलीय सहाय के सभी आयकर परिमंडल , वार्ड और 

आयक्त , पटियाला जिले जिनका मुख्यालय निम्न 
रेंज , पटियाला लिखित स्थानों पर है :-- - 

( i ) खन्ना 
(ii ) बरनाला 
( iii ) पटियाला 
( iv ) रोपड़ 

- - - - - - - - 


बशर्ते कि उन वार्डो, जिलों और परिमंडलों के संबंध - 
में क्षेत्राधिकार, जो समारत किये जा चुके हैं , अपीलीय सहायक 
आयुका के पास ही रहेंगे जो परिमंडलों, वार्डो और जिलों 
पर वर्तमान क्षेत्राधिकार रखते हैं , जिनके पास इन परिमंडलों , 
वाओं ओर जिलों के मामलों का वर्तमान में कर-निर्धारण 
किया जाता है । 
___ जहां कोई आयकर परिमंडल , वार्ड और जिला या 
उसका कोई भाग इस अधिसूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी 
अन्य रेंज में अंतरित कर दिया जाता है वहां उस आयकर 
परिमंडल , वार्ड या जिले या उसके किसी भाग में किये गये 
कर-निर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाली और इस अधिसूचना 
की तारीख से तत्काल पूर्व उस अपीलीय सहायक आयकर 
आयुषन के समक्ष विचाराधीन पड़ी अपीलें , जिसके अधिकार 
क्षेत्र से उस आयकर परिमंडल , वार्ड या जिला अथवा उसका 
कोई भाग अंतरित किया गया हो , इस अधिसूचना के लाग 
होने की तारीख से रेंज के उस अपीलीय सहायक आयकर 
आयुक्त को अंतरित की जायेंगी और उसके द्वारा उन पर 
कार्यवाही की जायेंगी, जिसके अधिकार क्षेत्र में उक्त परिमंडल , 
वार्ड या जिला या उसका कोई भाग अंतरित किया गया 
हो । 

जहां सभी परिमंडल वार्ड या जिले जिनका मुख्यालय 
किसी विशिष्ट स्थान पर है, किसी एक अपीलीय सहायक 
आयुक्त के सुपुर्द किए गये हों , वहां इन मुख्यालयों के परि . 
मंडलों, वार्डों और जिलों के संबंध में भी उन्हीं का क्षेत्रा 
धिकार होगा जो अब समाप्त भी हो चुके हों । 

यह अधिसूचना 15- 7-1983 से लागू होगी ।

7 को उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के भूतपूर्व इस्पात खान और कोयला ऊर्जा मंत्रालय ( कायला विभाग ) की अधिमचना 
स० का . आ० 1 5 39, तारीख 31 मार्च 1982 द्वारा उग अधिसूचना में मलग्न अन मची में विनिर्दिष्ट परिक्षेत्र में भमि का अर्जन 
करन के अपन आशय की सूचना दी थी ; 

और मक्षम प्राधिकारी न उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 8 के अनुमगण मे अपनी रिपोर्ट केन्द्रीय मरकार को दे दी है . 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार का पूर्वोक्न रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने और बिहार सरकार में परामर्श करने के पश्चात , यह समाधान 
हो गया है कि इमर्म मंनग्न अनुसूची में वणित 111 50 एकड़ ( लगभग ) या .15 . 1 : हैक्टर ( लगभग ) माप की भूमि का अर्जन 
किया जाएगा 

अत . अब केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम की धाग 9 वां उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तिया का प्रयोग करते हए यह 
घोषणा करती है कि उन अनुमुची में वणित 111 50 एकड़ ( लगभग ) या 15 . 12 हैक्टर ( लगभग ) माप की भूमि का 
अर्जन किया जाता है । 

___ इम अधिसूचना के अधीन आने वाले क्षेत्र के रखाक का निरीक्षण उपआयक्त हजारी बाग (बिहार ) के कार्यालय में या 
कोयला नियन्त्रक 1 कामिल हाउस स्ट्रीट , कलकता के कार्यालय में या मेन्टल कोलफील्ड लिमिटेप ( राजस्व अनुभाग ) दरभंगा

मंत्रालय में संयुक्त सचिव श्री जे० एन० कोल को श्री 
सीएम चतुर्वेदी के स्थान पर तत्काल तीन वर्ष की अवधि 
के लिए भारत अंतरराष्ट्रीय विमानपत्तन प्राधिकरण का 
अंशकालिक सदस्य नियक्त करती है ।

का० प्रा० 3079 : - चलचित्र ( सेंसर ) नियम , 
1958 के नियम 3 के साथ पठित चलचित्र अधिनियम , 
1952 की धारा 3 को उपधारा ( 1 ) के द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार, श्री विक्रम 
सिंह को केन्द्रीय फिल्म प्रमाणन बोर्ड के अंशकालिक अध्यक्ष 
के पद पर 8 - 7-- 83 ( अपरान ) से अगले आदेश तक 
नियुक्त करती है । 

[ फाइल संख्या 802/ 22/ 79-एफ० ( सी० )] 

के० एस० वेंकटारमण, अवर सचिव 


के कालम (i) में उल्लिखित अधिकारियों , जो राजपत्रित 
अधिकारी है , को उक्त अधिनियम के प्रयोजनार्थ सम्पदा 
अधिकारी नियुक्त करती है तथा उक्त अधिकारियों को 
यह निदेश देती है कि वे उक्त तालिका के कालम ( 2 ) में 
निदिष्ट संबंधित प्रविष्टि सरकारी स्थान के संबंध में उक्त 
अधिनियन द्वारा या के अंतर्गत सम्पदा अधिकारियों को प्रवत्त 
शक्तियों का , अपने क्षेत्राधिकारी की स्थानीय सीमाओं में , 
प्रयोग करें और सौंपे गये कर्तव्यों को पूरा करें ।

की बेदखली ) अधिनियम , 1971 के नियम 6 के अनुसरण में 
तथा भारत सरकार रेल मंत्रालय ( रेलवे बोर्ड ) की 15 जून , 
1973 की अधिसूचना सां० आ० संख्या 1856 के अतिक्रमण 
में , केन्द्रीय सरकार एसद्वारा नीचे तालिका के कालम ( ) 
में निदिष्ट राजपत्रित अधिकारियों को किसी भी समय 
या तो अपने ही प्रस्ताव पर या किसी ऐसे व्यक्ति जिसे 
सरकारी स्थान ( अप्राधिकृत अधिभोगियों की बेदखली ) 
अधिनियम , 1971 ( 1971 का 40 ) के अधीन नोटिस

इससे अपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषय के बारे में

औद्योगिक विवाद नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच 
विद्यमान है ।

4 . Shri D . K . Mukherjee for the management strenuously 
urged that there was 1o post to which he could be appointed . 
The point has no force . It is the management who 
hag right to create a post. It truy be noted that there aro 
two types of industrial digpute . Those about the negotiation 
of new and improve terms of employment (dispute of into 
cest ) and those about the inlerprotution , application and 
unforcement of oxisting terms ( disputo of right) . The pre 
Hent dispute 18 a dispute of interest. In such a cabo tho 
Industrial Tribunal can create new obligation and now 
rights between the partics . If there is no post , it is tho duty 
of the management in the facts and circumstances of the 
case to create a post for Radha Krishna Jana whom they 
appointed in 1971 and from whom thev have been taking 
service up -to - day and are likely to take service in futures 
Also , Morcover I think that by appointing him as night guard , 
It will be presumed that a poet of night guard was created 
at the site. So the contention has no merit ,

the following dispute to this Tribunal for adjudication :

Pandey , a junior subordinate staff to the post of 
Head Poon , ignoring the clair of S / Shri Ram 
Chandra Prasad and Prem Bahadur Chotri wica 
effect from 1 - 1 - 1982 18 justified ? If not, to what 
relief is the concerned workmen entitled ? " 


2 . The matter has been voluntarily and amicably actued 
between the parties on the following terms and conditions ; - 


" 1 . That the management will pay full wages with all 

allowances for the period 10 - 7 - 80 to 31- 1-81. 


2 . That the G . P . F . funds to the credit of the workman 

will be recommended for release , if not already 


paid . 


3 . That the Gratuity and pension payment as per rules 

admissiblo to the workman will also be recommend 
cd for payment, treating his service upto 31- 1 -81, 

for the purpose of this benchts .

putes demands raised by the workman so far , to his

tatc claim for any damages/ logs cost suffered or in

3 . The terms of settlement aforesaid are fair and proper 
and aro incorporated in this award and an award in tornis

which will govern the dispute referred to this Tribunal and

0 . P . SINGLA , Presiding Officer 
June 21, 1983 . 


2 . Tho previous Head Peon Bhowani Prasad Pandey was 
working in the Shambazar branch of ihe Grindlay Bank . 
Ho retired from service from 1 January 1982 . Undisputably 
he was the Head pcon , After his retirement the management 
entrusted the duty of tho Head peon to Dev Narayan Pandey 
on permanent basis whose appointment has beon challenged 
in the instant case mainly on the ground that the appoint 
mcat was not made on the basis of seniority . Sri Ajit Banor 
jce , the President of the Union , appearing for the union has 
raised several contentions challenging the appointment of 
Deo Narayan Pandey as head peon . His first contention is 
that the management should have a senior subordinate staff 
to the post of head peon on the basis of geniority and Ram 
Chandra Prasad being admittedly the senior -most sub - staff by 
about 11 years to Deo Narayan Pandey should bave been 
posted to the post of Head peon which carries & social 
allowance . In my opinion tho contention has no merit ja 
the facts and circumstances of this case . Admittedly there 
is no rulo or regulation or any provision in any bipartito 
settlement that seniority must be considered in the matter 
of Appointment of bead peon , In absence of any such rule , 
I think , the nanagemont can appoint any person as head 
peon whom they consider suitable . The matter has been 
sottled by a decision in Grindlays Bank Ltd . and Central 
Goyt , Industrial Tribugal , 1978 II LLJ 462 (Cni ). In that 
case His Lordship Sabyasachi Mukherjee observed at page 
466 : " In my opinion , it is indisputable that in absence of 
any rule controlling promotion or selection , the management 
has the power to select or promote anyone it cboobes , Tho 
management however is not free to act in a manner which 
can be described as malafide or victimisation or unfair labour 
practico ." I may also quote here the following observation 
of Justice Sinha in thọ caso of Royal Calcutta Golf Club v . 
Third Industrial Tribunal & Ors . , 1960 I LLJ 464 (Cal.) et 
pago 467 : " A person has tho right to reorganise his business 
in any fashion hd likes , for the purpose of economy or con 
yenience , and nobody is entitled to tell him how he should 
conduct his business . The only limitation is that he should 
do it bonafide and not for the purpose of victimising his 
employee . . . . " In the present care admittedly there is no 
rulo as already stated , governing the appointment of head 
poon . It is clear therefore that the management had power 
to select any person of their choice for that nost whom they 
considered suitable for tho job . The only thing to be seen 
is whether the act of the management can be described AS 
malafide or victimisation or unfair labour practice ,

a latge number of senior persons and so malice in law and

case of promotion . It is a case of selection . The present 
cage is covered by the principice laid down in the case of 
Grindlays Bank Ltd . v . Central Govt . Industrial Tribunal, 
1978 ( 2 ) LLJ 462 (Cal. ). In that caso also the bank had 
posted a junior staff to the post or drafiry overlooking the 
claim of a senior sub - staff . It was held that the Tribunal 
had no justification to interfere with the selection of the 
management . The argument that the management bad acted 
in a malafide manner or in a manner which could be described 
as unfair labour pracice or victimjaation was rejected because 
there was no evidence that the management had acted in 
order to curb any trade union activties or in order to punish 
any workman for his trado unlon activities. Here also thero 
is no such evidence . On the contrary , the evidence is other 
wise which I have already referred to . It is to be noticed 
that noither Ram Chandra Prasad nor Chetri has examined 
himself. Tho union has got examined any witness . There is 
thus no oral evidence on behalf of the union . The union 
bas not placed any documentary evidence to show that the 
managmement acted in a malafide manner . They have filed 
three documents Exts . W1. W2 and W3. By Ext. W2 Ram 
Chandra Prasad was asked to officiate from 6th June, 1983 
until Deo Narain Pandey returned . By Ext . W 1 Prem Babadur 
Chetri was asked to officiate for two days from 7th June, 
1983 to 8th June, 1983 . Ext . W - 3 is the letter of the manage 
ment to ALC ( C ), Calcutta dated 16th February , 1982 stating 
that they had right to select Deo Naroid Prasad as head peon . 
By Ext . M - 1 the Union raised the dispute with the manage 
ment. Thus there is no evidence on record . Oral or docu 
mentary , of any mala fides or victimisation or of unfair 
Jabour practice . I thereforo hold that the Union has failed to 
establish malafide or victimisation or unfair labour practice . 


3 . Sri Ajit Banerjee argues that the act of the management 
was malafide of unfair labour practice and victimisation 
because the act of supersession was done only to humiliate 
the Grindlays Bank Employees Association whose members 
are Ram Chandra Prasad and Prem Babadur Chetri where 
2 : Dco Naraia Pandoy the younger brother of Bhawani 
Prasad Pandoy (tho retired head peon ) is a member of the 
rival union , namely , the Grindlays Bank Employees Upion 
which according to the bank is a recognised union . It is 
urged that this Union enjoyed favour of the branch Manager 
of the Shambazar branch of the bank , Sri Banerjee points 
out that Ram Chandra Prasad admittedly officiated in the 
pogt of bead peod on 15 or 16 occasions in between 1976 
and 1981 and that Prem Bahadur Chotsi also had officiated 
on 2 or 3 OCCAşsiong (vide MW - 1 , P . Nandy) whereas Dco 
Narain Pandey had never oficiated or acted as head peon 
and so the management unjustifiably showed naked favouri 
tism to Deo Narain Pandey ignoring the rightful claim of 
Ram Chandra Prasad and Prem Bahadur Chetri. My atten 
tion was drawn to the letted of these two peong ( Annexuro 
A dated 2 January 1981 and Annexure B dated 12 January 
1982 respectively ) expressing their grievances in this regard . 
In my opinion there is no substance in these contention , The 
very fact that in spite of the presence of Dco Narain Pandey 
in the office , the management assigned tho duty of head pcon 
on Officiating bagig to Ram Chandra Prasad about 15 or 16 
times and to Prem Bahadur Chetri 2 or 3 timeg goes to show 
that thero was no malice on the part of the management , 
MW 1 P . Nandy, the Manager çf Shambazar tranch of the 
bank in his evidence has made the position very clear . He 
has stated that he had assigned such duty to them because it 
was only on temporary basis and not on permanent basis 
and in doing so he had also regard for seniority . Now , tho 
present appointment was admittedly made on permaneni 
basis . Regarding Ram Chandra Prasad MW 1 has deposed 
that he had some chronic ailment, that he wag not co -opera 
tive and sometimes questioned to the allotted job to the 
management staff and on one occasion he did not even re 
move the books of account to book -salo which was his nor 
mal duty without intimating the officer , In his cross - exami 
nation the witness has said that he had entrusted Ram Chan 
dra Prasad to act as head peon in place of Deo Narain 
Pandey from 6 June . 1983 (see Ext. W 2 also ) but he fall 
gick and so he had to assign that duty to Prem Bahadur 
Chetri for two days (ace Ext W - 1 ) during the jilness of Ram 
Chandra Prasad . Regårding Prom Bahadur Chetri, he has 
deposed that ho Wan appointed as Watchman and could not 
properly understand the instructions given by the officers 
and that he was short of hearing. The witnesi has clearly 
said that the performance of these people was being closely 
observed by him . As regards Deo Narain Pandey , he has 
deposed that ho has the initiative to do the job , he was co 
opcrativo and possessed good health , that whenever any work 
Was entrusted to him , he performed the work perfoctly and 
to the natisfaction of the officer , In cross - examination he 
hag stated that Deo Narain Pandey was attending to the 
clearing job from Shambazar branch and was also doing 
collection peon s job and other outside job as required by 
the branch . The union took out in his cross -examination 
that he had not isoued any letter to Ram Chandra Praged 
for non - compliance with management s order on one occu 
sion but the witnesg has given tho ronly that they were sym 
pathetic to him and no letter was jured . In ny oninion thn 
witness is reliable . His evidence is convincing and truthful. 
I rely on his evidence . Now , in view of this evidence . Can 
it be paid that the managenient acted malafide ? The answer 
must be in the negative . It is a well -established principle of 
law that the burden of establisbing matafleden lien very heavily 
on the person who alleges it . Considering all the alligations 
and gubmissions of the Union in regard thereto . I think , 
they cannot be considered as sufficient to establish mal119 
animus . On the contrary , the evidence of manager . MW 1 , 
Sri P . Nandy clearly proves that the management arted hona 
Ado considering all the prog and cons of the matter . It was 
not an act of victimisation because neither Ram Chandra 
nor Prem Bahadur Chetri was made victim of any infair and 
Arhitrary action . One was suffering from chronis ailment. 
another was short of hearing . Not only that, their perfor 
mance was closely helne observed by the branch manager 
MW 1 P . Nandy. Srl Banerjee referred to the decision in 
Workmen of M / s . Williamson Magor & Co Ltd . v . M /H 
Williamson Magor & Co . Ltd ., 1982 Lah IC 33 : ATR 1982 
SC 78, but that was a case of unlıktified promotions of 


3 . Srl A . Banerjed next argued that suitability of Deo Narain 
for the job of head peon could not be judged when he had 
never acted or officiated as such , In my opinion the argument 
is not valid , Capability or suitability of a person for a parti 
cular Job can , I think , be judged from other performances 
of his and also from other factors as well , for example , from 
willingness to obey , taking initiative etc ., though the parti 
cular work for which he is selected has not been performed 
by him earlier. So , the point has not force . 


4 , Sri Banerjee next urged that the Bank has not followed 
their own usage , custom , convention and practice in the case 
of posting of head peon for filling up vacancies on the basis 
of seniority . Ho drew my attention to para 10 of the written 
statement of the Union where some names of sub - staff who 
were appointed as head peong in Calcutta main branch ( 19 
Notaji Subhan Road) and Chowringhce Road branch . Suffico 
to say that the Bank hag not accepted those statements and no 
one from the side of the union has come to the witness box 
to prove them . On a mero statement of this type and without 
any evidenco on record it cannot be held that it was the 
lisage , custom , convention and practice of the bank to fill 
un vacancies in the posts of head peong on the basis of 
çaniority . My attention was also drawn to the evidence in 
cross - cxamination of MW - 1 , where he said that in Assirning 
the officiating duty to Ram Chandra Prasad or Prem Bahadur 

horri he had regard to seniority but the witness has said 
that it was merely on tomporary basis . That cannot prove 
custom or practice . Tho contention is rejected . 


A mero tateno come to the us and no 


any evidence . 


5 . In the result , I hold that the action of the management 
AF Grindlays Bank , plc, Calcutta in posting Deo Narain 
Pandoy a Junior gubordinate staff to the post of head peon 
ignoring the claim of Ram Chantra Prasad and Prem Bahadur 
Chetri with effect from 18t January , 1982 is jugtiled . It 
follows that the workmon concerned are not entitled to any 
relief. This is my award .

to accept retrenchment compensation to save their families 
from starvation . The workmen also alleged that most of 
the concerned workmen were transferred to different sites after 
receiving compensation . The Conciliation Officer being dis 
satisfied with the conduct of the management referred the 
matter to the Government of India and accordingly this 
reference has been made for adjudication on the quesiion 
raised in the schedule of the reference , 


3 . The management of Cemindia Ltá . , Calcutta has admitted 
that in March , 1980 the company obtained from M / S . TISCO 
a contract for construction , raising of Staple Shafts etc . at 
Bhelatand colliery . The workmen concerned were engaged 
in the above construction work . The construction work at 
Bielatand colliery were nearing its completion when this 
dispute was raised by the concerned workmen . The company 
submitted its comments to the workmen representation 
daiad 16 - 10 - 81 by their own letter dated 9 - 11- 81. The case 
of the company is that most of the retrenched workmen , 
barring a few whose names appear in the anexure to the 
schedule of the reference , approached the management and 
settle their differences amicably and accepted the payment 
of all their dues in full and final settlement of the claims. 
The company s case is that notices of retrenchment were 
issued only when the work at Bhelatand was about to be 
completed and according to Section 25FFA and 25FFF of 
the I. D . Act , 1947 the employer had ample right to close 
down an undertaking set up for construction work and to 
retrench the workers engaged therein after following the 
provisions of S . 25F of the I. D . Act, 1947 . The company 
followed the procedure as envisaged in S . 25F of the LD . 
Ast and therefore the action of the management in retrench 
ing the concerned workmen cannot be said to be illegal and 
unitstified : With regard to the order of the Conciliation 
Officer on a petition under $ .33 of the I. D . Act, 1947 , the 
company s stand is that the Conciliation Officer has mis 
directed himself in assuming the jurisdiction and power in 
the matter of directing the company not to retrench these 
workmen . 


2 . From the annexure attached to the schedule of this 
reference it will appear that they are 68 workmen . Their 
case is that they have been working as employees of Messi s 
Cernindia Company Ltd , previously known as Messrs Cemeil 
tation Co . According to the workmen they have been working 
in permanent and perenial nature of jobs. According to them 
all of them put in more than 240 days attendence in each 
calender year . The company takes contract and work 
order of Shaft sinking in differenti mines coal mines and 
other mines . The practice followed by this company is that 
as soon as the contract work in a praticular mine is completed 
the employees of the company are served with a notice 
of retrenchment and called again when another work is 
obtained . This is said to be an unfair labour practice with 
the result that the workmen are deprived of their permanency 
and other benefits inspite of the fact that these workmen can 
be usefully and gainfully employed for ar " indefinite period . 
The workmen can also be transferred from one place to 
another. where the work is in progress . But this is not 
done. The company had obtained contract in Sijua , Bhelatand 
of Messrs TISCO : On completion of work the management 
issued retrenchment notices on " 14 - 12 81 proposing to re 
trench the concerned workmen w . e . f . 26 - 12 - 81. These work 
men raised an industrial dispute before the Assistant Labour 
Commissioner ( C ) Dhanbad and thc conciliation proceeding 
was started . Inspite of the conciliation proceeding the 
company retrenched these workmen with the result that an 
application under S . 33 of the I. D . Act , 1947 was filed : 
before the Conciliation Officer ( C ) challenging the proposed 
retrenchment of the workmen on the ground that the 
management had no darth of pork because another coniract 
work of the management was doing on at Bhelatand site , 
Putkee Balihari Integrated Project and other sites . The work 
men drew the attention of the conciliation officer to the fact 
that the ufficers of the company were doing contract jobs 
in Blalgora and also in Putkee and Balihari Integrated Project 
in the names of their wives , sons, sisters . etc , and were noi 
prepared to accommodate these concerned workmen in the 
Jom which were going on . It was also urged that the 
management changed their service conditions by retrenching 
them during the pendency of the conciliation proceeding 
without taking prior permission of the conciliation Officer . 
The workmen also prayed before the conciliation officer 
to make an enquiry into the allegations by visiting the spot. 
The learned conciliation officer at the request of the workers 
visited the above sites and found that the company had 
engaged contract workers in the permanent nature of jobs 
below the rates of minimum wages . The concerned workmea 
prayed before the conciliation officer to direct the manage 
ment to remove the illegal and moiivated retrenchment order 
and to transfer the concerned workmen in the above mentioned

contract for shaft sinking was entered into between the 
Cemindia as shown by document. Ext . M -25 dated 12 - 12 -79 :

was not permanent. He has admitted that the concerned . 
workmen had been working with the Cemindia from before

in this construction project. He has sa :d that the construction

14 - 12 -81. One of them is marked Ext . M7. The statutory

Bharat Coking Coal Lid . Thereafter the company recruited

whenever the company gets any contract work , the company

7 . Before I go to discuss the evidence on merits of the 
case I would like to discuss or a technical objection iaken 
at the time of giving on behalf of the management that 
this reference for adjudication has not been properly mauc . 
Tue contention is that a dispute of this naiure is not by any 
union nor under S .2A of the 1. D . Act, 1947. It will appear 
that faced with the situation of retrenchment these concerned 
workmen who are 68 in numbers jointly filed a represen . ation 
before the Assistant Labour Commissioner ( C ) Dhanbad . 
It is this representation which turned into a dispute when 
in spite of the direction of the Assistant Labour Commissioner 
( C ) these workmen were retrenched . It will mean that ihe en 
tire body of workmen facing retrenchment raised this dispute 
with a community of interest. This is a basic position which 
cannot be ignored . Then again it is urged that before this 
Tribunal there has been no proper representation and no 
proper authority has been given to Shri D . Mukherjee to 
represent the workmen . The contention of the workmen is 
that almost all the workmen have been attending this court 
on various dates and Shri D . Mukherjee , Advocate of course 
pleaded their case . There is no doubt some technical lacuna . 
Shri D . Mukherjee has however argued that a similar thing can 
be said about the management. MW - 1 Shri Raghavan in his 
evidence has adrzitted Cemindia is controlled by a Board of 
Directors and the Board of Directors gave him authority to 
sign the written statemeint on behalf of the company . Shri 
Ragbavan has also said that he has not brought the autho 
rity to be produced in the court. Thereafter such an authority 
has not been filed . Shri D . Mukherjee has , therefore , argued 
that the written statement of the management should be out 
right rejected and the case of the workmen should be decided 
ex parte . Faced with such a situation the question of main 
tainability of this reference nas not been seriously pleaded 
before me on behalf of the management and similarly Shri 
D . Mukherjee agreed that the case should be considered on 
merits . In view of the submissions made before me hy the 
parties I have to hold that this reference is maintainable .

8 . In considering the merits I would point out ihat I have 
placed the evidence in this case on tehalf of both the parties , 
and the facts are no longer in dispute , Cemindia are no doubt 
contractors and their main contract is of shaft sinking in 
coal and other mines . They do specialised work on contract 
basis . They have their head office at Calcutia and whatever 
Project they take for execution they have to keep their 
staff at the Project site , Shri Raghavan has admitted that 
these concerned workmen are technical people and experien 
ced in their jobs and some of them are in the employment 
of Cemindia for a pretty long time. So this case of the work . 
men is admitted by the management. The workmen have 
alleged , and this is the positive case of the management that 
they recruit afresh ther workmen as and when the new 
contract is obtained , and whenever the contract is finished 
the workmen are retrenched . For this purpose reliance has 
been placed on the provisions of S . 25FFA and S .25FFF of 
the I. D . Act, 1947 . There is no lispute with regard to the 
principleas nobody can question the althority of the manage 
ment to retrench a workman if no work can be provided 
to these workmen . The princila i ! ciicroliment is an inte 
gral part of industrial lali and the basis ... ar in the principle 
the retrenchment is that if the employer cannot provide any , 
work to its workmen , he has tofollow the provisions of retrench 
ment as provided in the Act . In this case the management s 
contention is that the work at Bhelatand had ended or was 
about to end and so the retrenchment notices were served . 
The workmen do not challenge the right of the management

12 . Thus, having considered all aspects of the casc , the 
reference is answered accordingly , 


I hold that the action of the management of Cemindia 
CO ., any Limited at and P . O . Bhelatand (Dhanbad ) in 
issuing notice on 14th December , 1981, for retreaching their 
workmen listed in the anncaurd of the schedule from 
26 - 12 - 1981, in not regularising them in company s service , 
and in not transferring them to South Balihori and Bhalgora 
sites , is not justified . Consequently , thợ concerned workmen 
are therefore entitled to be regularised in company s service 
from the date of retrenchment i. c . 26 - 12 - 1981 and they are 
cntitled to all the buck wages and other emoluments from 
26 - 12 - 1981 .

The contention on behalf of the workmen is that if they

9 . The question arises as to whether the management of 
the company in order to break the continuity of service ure 
entitled under industrial law to effect an order of ietrench 
ment. On behalf of the workinen it has been said that this 
is an unfair labour practice . According to them each one of 
the workmen have put in more than 240 days ultendence 
in a single calendar year and in this madrer some of the 
have been working such as WW2 since 1964 . Such workinen 
in spite of their long years of service have not been regularis 
ed . The contention before me on behalf of the workman 
is that even after these workmea arc regularised in service, 
the management should have still the power to retrench theni 
under $ . 23N of the I . D . Act if no work is available . The 
workman in absence of continuity of service have to lose 
their increment, gratuity , etc . which is permissible linder 
industrial law and iſ every time they have to face re 
trenchment thcir future will always remain in dark . It has 
also been urged on bchalf of ihc workmen that the company 
should not be permitted to resoit to retrenchment in spite of 
the fact that work is available . It has also been contended 
that faced with this siuation the menagemen has started pick 
and choose method so that during the pendency of this 
reference two of the concerned workmen have been regularised 
in service . It cannot be therefore 5 ? id that the management 
on principle could not regularise these concerned workmen . 
I have to agree that the grounds advanced on behalf of the 
workmicn against retrenchnent and regularisation of these 
concerned workmen are sound and cannot be ignored . 

10 . In the last resort it has been urgct on behalf of the 
management that these concerned workmen had been employ 
ed for a particular project and with the ending of the 
project they have no right to continue in the job , Shri 
Raghuvan deposed in his cvidence about the existence of 
separate establishment for each project . It has not been subs 
tantiated by any document filed on behalf of the company . 
In fact the contract was entered into between Cemindia , 
Calcutta and TISCO . The appointment letters were issued 
by Cenrendia to these workmen . So there is no cvidence of 
existence of separate establishment apart from the establish 
ment of Cemindia . It cannot br therefore said that the 
tstablishinent which appointed these workinen ceased to exist 
after the contract was completed . The simple position is 
that these workmen have been workmen of Cemindia and 
the retrenchment order was pulled by Cenrindia in spite of 
the fact that retrenchment was not necessary in view of the 
fact that construction work was going on at two sites where 
these workmen could he easily eninloved and in fact have 
been employed soon after these concerned workmen accepted 


This is my award .

taking any recourse to any legal formalitics of holding any 
domestic enquiry for any allcged misconduct of overstay or 
without fulfilling the conditions precedent to relrenchment of 
a workman prescribed in Section 25F of the Industrial Diy . 
putes Act 1947 . The reference is therefore , answered in 
favour of the concerned workman and It is held that the 
action of the management in refusing employment to the con 
cerned workman , Bandhan Barhi, pick miner with effect 
from 11 - 7 - 1979 is unjustified and the concerned workinan is 
entitled to be reinstated with effect from the said dute with 
all back wages . In the circumstance of the case , there will be 
no order as to cost .

12 . The concerned workinen had received the charge 
shects and replied to the cburgo -sheets . They denied their

was that since they took active part in the activities of 
their union and were office bearers of the union , the manage 
ment was determined to oust them from service. The enquiry 
started on 12 -4 - 1980 and concluded on 23 - 7 -80 . The enquiry 
officer submitted the report on 9 -2 -81 le , after a lapse of 
7 months . The contention on behalf of the concerned work 
men is that after submission of the enquiry report the manage 
ment felt that the offences alleged were not of such nature and 
furthermore were not proved , so its to remove the concerned 
workmen from service . They further contended that duo 
to thig dificulty felt by the management , they issued tansfer 
letters on 4th June , 1981 whereunder Shri A . K . Banerjeo 
was transferred to Orissa arca, Shri Narayan Gope to 
Bharkhana area , Shri Keshav Singh Yadav to Singroli area , 
Shri S , S . Gupta to Dakra area and Shri K , K , Chatterjee to 
Ranchi. They were also released by the same order . Blhar 
Collicry Kamgarh Union served a strike notice challenging 
the illegal and malafide transfer order. The Conciliation

Simlota , who held the conclliation proceeding. The concilia 
tion ended in failure and the failure report was received in 
thc Ministry on 19 - 9 -81. The 2 : onagement fogued charge 
sheet for violating the transfer order on 6 - 11 - 81 i.e . after

26 . The moive of the management in this transfer are to 
pack this workmen away A8 8000 89 possible and so by tho 
transfer letters they were immediately received without affor 
ding them as Opportunity to put up any representation . 
of BC. K , Union so that the trade to territoriso other members 
of B . C .K , Union so that the trade union activities of B . C .K . 
Union could be stopped . The standing order requires that 
transfer can be effected for exigency of work . In the instant 
case the grounds for transfor is for administrative reasons as 
shown by the letters of transfer . The exigencies of work 
means that the places of posting demanded Workmen of a 
particular typo due to efficiency reasons. In this case the 
management has not shown any rason why the services of 
thcąd concerned workmen were more necessary at the places 
of posting to which they were transferred . Insctad they were 
transferred because of their presenco at Kathara area would 
ha detrimental to the interest of the administration . It is 
clear that the reasons behind transfer cannot be said to be 
congistent with the provision , of Standing order relating to 
guch transfer . 


27 . The above discussions will show that the dismissal of 
these concerned workmen passed on the two sets of charge 
sheets cannot be said to be justifiod . The management should 
have borne in mind that the concerned workmen against such 
transfer had raised an industrial digoute and the failure re 
port had been already sent to the Government of India . In 
fart a reference has already been made to this court and the 
dignute which is nending for adiudication The mana toment 
could have waited for a decision of the Tribunal in the 
transfer matters instead of bastening to dismiss the concerned 
workmen for violation of the transfer order, 


24 . In a case like thiş it is pertinent to note that thero 
should be a line of domarcation between the upion activities 
WluLb aro premissible and such activities detrimental to the 
interest of tho work which tho workmen are expected to do . 
The purpose of the union is to product the logitimate right of 

vorkmen and in course of tho samo they are entitled to 
ventilate their grievances in a procession and they can even 
resort to strike as a mothod of enforcing their legitimate de 
mand . In this cas processions were taken out and oven strike 
wag resorted in order to secure the legitimate demand of the 
wcopora , Thoro was a strike and some actions wero 
taken by tho swcoper8 which coated a lot of bitterness in the 
minds of the officers of the management. This led to several 
actions including a proceeding under S . 107 Cr. P . C . and a 
criminal case against the leaders of the movements , ice , execu 
tivo of Bihar Colliery Kamgarh Union , I may point out 
that Shri A , K . Banerjee is the President of B . C . K . Union , 
Shri Keshav Singh Yaday and Shri Nagrayan Gope ure Secre 
taries of the union , Shri K . C . Chatterjee is Vice President of 
the Union and Shri S S Gupta is the Executive Committee 
Member of the Union , None of the sweepers have been in 
volved in any of the proceeding either departmental or cri 
minal. The contention on behalf of the workmen jo that ever 
inco B . C . K . Union wag formed , tho management is to crush 
the leaders of the union , Now we cannot ignore this fact as 
a cause of bitterness between the officers of the management 
and the leaders of B . C . K . Vnion . I have tried to scrutinise 
the evidence adduced in this case to show that there is no con 
vincing ev dence to hold them gulty of any of the charges fre 
ed against the concerned workmen , In the context of bitterness, 
it is likely that some of the concerned workmen may have 
uttered objectionablo slogans and showed militant attitude . 
But neither they nor the processionists adopted any violent 
attitude, I am also confident in my mind that if the officers 
of the management had approached the executives of the 
union in a conciliatory manner , and had attended to the grie 
yances raised by them . such a situation which led to aggregive 
attitudo against the Sub Area Manager and the Officers of the 
management would have been avoided . Suffice it to say that 
the charges in respect of the first domestic enguiry would 

within the AC0DC of legitimate union Activities ond so 
the charges could not be considered to he crious enough to 
warrant tho dismissal of the concerned workmon . 


28 . As a result of the above discussions the reference is 
answored accordingly , 


The action of the management of Central Coalfields Ltd is 
not justified in dismissing S / Shri K .S . Yadav , A . K . Banerjco, 
Narayan Gope , K . K , Chatterjee and S .S . Gupta (by two dis 
missal orders). Consequently , the concerned workmen should 
bo deemed to be in the service of Kathara colliery of Central 
Coalfields Ltd . from the date of their dismissal. They are also 
entitled to their back wages and other emoluments from the 
date of dismissal till they aro reinstated in their job . 


This is my award

APPEARANCES : 


3 . This cane has been board under Section 11A of the 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 . It is settled law that the 
tribunal has undoubted power to reapprise the evidence for 
itself and to come to its own conclusion , but, as observed in 
Workmen of Fire Stone and Rubber Co , v The Manage 
ment, 1973 Lab IC 851 in para 48 at p . 869 , " If a proper 
enquiry is conducted by an employer and a correct finding 
arrived at regarding the misconduct , the tribunal even thougb 
it has now power to differ from the conclusions arrived at 
by the management, will have to give very cogent reagona 
for not accepting the view of the cmployer. Further hy 
holding a proper enquiry , the employer will also escape the 
chargo of having acted arbitrarily or mala fido ", Keeping 
this principle in view let 118 sec whether there are very 
cogent reasons for not accepting the view of the Enquiry 
Officer . 


On behalf of Employers - Sri A , Choudhury, Counsel, 

with Sri M . N . Kar , Advocate . 


On behalf of Workmen . - Sri S. Roy, Advocate .

में एक श्रम न्यायाल्य गठित किया है जिसका न्याय क्षेत्राधिकार 
पंजाब , हिमाचल प्रदेश , हरियाणा , जम्मू और कश्मीर 
राज्यों तथा चण्डीगढ़ संघ राज्य क्षेत्र के ऊपर होगा 


अत : अब केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्योगिक विषाद अधिनियम 
1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 33 ख की उपधारा 
( i ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए उम्त आवेदनों 
से संबंधित कार्यवाहियों को उक्त श्रम न्यायालय मई दिल्ली 
से वापस लेती है और उन्हें उक्त श्रम न्यायाल्य पन्डीगढ़ 
को अन्तरित करती है । उक्त न्यायाल्य उन पर उसी 
प्रक्रम से कार्यवाही प्रारम्भ करेंगे जिससे वे उन्हें अन्तरित 
की गई है और विधि के अनुसार उनका निपटान करेंगे ।

का०मा० 3108: केन्द्रीय सरकार की यह राय है कि इससे 
उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषय के बारे में मैसर्स 
एस सी कं . लि . रामागुम्छम प्रभाग-III के प्रबंधतंत्र से 
संबंधित एक औद्योगिक विवाद नियोजकों और उनके कर्म 
कारों के बीच विद्यमान है ; 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णायन 
के लिए निवेशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

अत: अब केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 
1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) की धारा 7-क और धारा 10 
की उप-धारा ( i ) के खंड ( प ) द्वारा प्रद से शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए, एक औद्योगिक अधिकरण गठित करती

का० आ० 3118 . ----केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत 
होता है कि मेसर्स सी० काठी रेसन , टिम्बर मपेंट , थीलागार, 
पुरम अम्बासमुद्रम , तमिलनाडु तथा इसके प्रशासन कार्यालय , 
1 / 31 ए, पापानास मेन रोड, अम्बसमुद्रम तथा शाखाएं 
( i) 240 ए, मेन रोड. विक्रम सिंगपुरम (ii ) 9, सरमाजी 
रोड, कल्लीदाईकुरीची तथा फैक्ट्री श्रम सं० 538/ 31 26, 
मन्नार कोइल गांव सहित नामक स्थापन के सम्बद्ध नियोजक 
और कर्मचारियों की बहुसंख्या इस बात पर सहमत हो गई 
है कि कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि और प्रकीर्ण उबंध अधिनियम ,
